# Evil Eye Collection: sales frame prompts (10 per listing)

One image per prompt, square 1:1, 2048 px. Reference: a tight crop of that listing's approved hero, the piece only.

## R01 · Two Tone Evil Eye Ring

Colour world: terracotta #B95B45, accent cobalt blue, skin light warm.

### Frame 01 · Hero on a paper wave

```
Etsy thumbnail hero: a sheet of terracotta paper curls in one soft wave across the top of the image over a floor of terracotta paper one shade lighter; this ring (a ring with a flat round disc on top, 8.4 mm across, a little narrower than the index fingernail, never larger, framed by a thin raised yellow gold collar that holds it flush, not domed; the collar is 1.8 mm high and sits on a band 2 mm wide and 1.4 mm thick with a flat inner side. Each part is entirely one metal: the band, the thin raised collar around the disc and the closed back behind it are polished yellow gold; the disc inside the collar is polished unplated white gold, a soft warm grey metal with mirror reflections. The face of the disc shows four flat zones from the edge inward: the yellow gold collar, 0.8 mm wide; a ring of cobalt blue enamel (#17368C), 1.5 mm wide, that meets the collar directly with no other metal line between them; a flat ring of white gold, 0.9 mm wide; and a round centre dot of cobalt blue enamel, 2 mm across. The enamel is flush with the metal, flat and glossy, never domed; closed, solid, flat yellow gold back) stands upright on the paper floor, its top facing the camera, seen from slightly above, in sharp focus at the centre with generous empty space around it, one clean soft shadow under the curl; one short sprig of olive leaves lies far in a corner, out of focus. No people and no hands in the image. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: terracotta (#B95B45) and its slightly lighter and deeper shades only, matte paper, linen and silk surfaces; the backdrop is never white, cream or grey; the small out-of-focus olive sprig is the only other colour. Shot on a Canon 50 mm lens at f/2.8, ISO 400, soft directional studio key light from the upper left with one clean defined shadow, fine 35 mm film grain, true colour, no heavy retouching. The ring must be an exact copy of the ring in the reference image: same outline and proportions, same enamel colours and cell layout, same thin polished rims, the same metal on every part (the band, the collar and the back in yellow gold, the flat ring between the two cobalt blue cells in white gold); do not redesign it, do not add charms, motifs or stones; take only the ring from the reference, never its paper background, camera angle or framing. The enamel is perfectly flat, glossy and flush with the metal, never domed, no gradient, no shading, no painted detail, no stones; no eyelashes, no eyelid line, no realistic iris. No text, no numbers, no logo, no watermark, no ruler. Square 1:1.
```

## N01 · Two Tone Evil Eye Necklace

Colour world: apricot #F0B48A, accent sky blue, skin medium olive.

```

Each part is entirely one metal: the thin raised collar around the disc, the closed back behind it, a small tube hidden behind the top edge of the disc and the fine 1.2 mm cable chain are polished yellow gold; the disc inside the collar is polished unplated white gold, a soft warm grey metal with mirror reflections. The face of the disc shows five flat zones from the edge inward: the yellow gold collar, 0.8 mm wide; a ring of cobalt blue enamel (#17368C), 1.6 mm wide, that meets the collar directly with no other metal line between them; a flat ring of white gold, 0.9 mm wide; a ring of pale sky blue enamel (#8CC3E8), 1.6 mm wide; and a round white gold centre dot, 1.7 mm across. The fine 1.2 mm cable chain passes through the hidden tube, so it disappears behind the top edge of the disc and no loop shows above it. The enamel is flush with the metal, flat and glossy, never domed; closed, solid, flat yellow gold back) lies face up on the paper floor with the round disc at the bottom and the chain leaving it as one single strand whose two halves run straight up and apart like the arms of a letter V and leave the image at the top edge, never doubled, never coiled, never a second loop, seen from slightly above, in sharp focus at the centre with generous empty space around it, one clean soft shadow under the curl; one short sprig of olive leaves lies far in a corner, out of focus. No people and no hands in the image. Colour world, identical in every image of this listing: apricot (#F0B48A) and its slightly lighter and deeper shades only, matte paper, linen and silk surfaces; the backdrop is never white, cream or grey; the small out-of-focus olive sprig is the only other colour. The necklace must be an exact copy of the necklace in the reference image: same outline and proportions, same enamel colours and cell layout, same thin polished rims, the same metal on every part (the collar, the back and the chain in yellow gold, the flat ring between the cobalt blue and sky blue rings and the round centre dot in white gold); do not redesign it, do not add charms, motifs or stones; take only the necklace from the reference, never its paper background, camera angle or framing. The enamel is perfectly flat, glossy and flush with the metal, never domed, no gradient, no shading, no painted detail, no stones; no eyelashes, no eyelid line, no realistic iris.
